# Rare GBA sound driver - decoded data dump (DKC1 / DKC2 / DKC3)

Generated from the three European ROMs in `GBAAudioLab/RARE/`. Companion to *Rare GBA sound driver - technical reference*.
All addresses are GBA bus addresses. Field meanings are in the technical reference, section 7.

Conventions: **len** = sample length in bytes (`end - start + 1`), **loop** = loop length in bytes (looping samples repeat the last `loop` bytes), 
**A/D/S/R** = the 0-99 envelope parameters, **fine** = cents, **bend** = pitch-bend range in semitones, **LFO** = vibrato rate in Hz (phase step x 60 / 2^24), **mod** = vibrato depth in cents at mod wheel 127. 
Mode 1/2 = one-shot/looped (music), 3/4 = one-shot/looped (SFX).

---
